# Internal source coverage and exported-symbol index

[Main catalogue](README.md) · Source version **0.2.34**, commit `4b9488732a560797446ee677517adbd6581c8d60`. Static source inventory; not runtime verification.

## Selected backend modules

768 non-test modules were indexed across services, repositories, security audit, payment, configuration and selected middleware. Exported Go functions/methods below are navigation aids, not additional product-feature claims. Multiple receivers can legitimately use the same method name. Private helpers, generated Ent code and tests are not listed as exported features.
